The agent will run the command, capture the output, and provide a clear summary focusing on errors, warnings, and key information. &lt;example&gt; Context: The user has just written some code and wants to check if it compiles successfully. user: "Run 'npm run build' and tell me if there are any… ([blennster/dotfiles](https://github.com/blennster/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shell/dot-config/opencode/agents/subtask-runner.md))
